        {
            "id": "2e127d29-c32c-4c2b-b0ff-fbf4256d0a9f",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1792072216.049468,
            "update_time": 1792072236.154178,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "For your budget of **₹1.5 crore, I’d start with Sectors 76, 77 and 78 in Central Noida**, focusing on ready-to-move 3BHK flats near the Aqua Line metro. This keeps you reasonably close to your office in Sector 63 while giving you access to established residential societies, schools, parks and everyday shopping.\n\nI’d prioritise a well-maintained, already-occupied society over a brand-new project with uncertain possession or registration.\n\n## My shortlist to investigate first\n\n<text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">These are candidate societies, not confirmed available flats. The ₹1.5 crore budget and actual asking prices need to be checked against individual listings.</text>\n\n<box gap={4}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Sethi Max Royal Sector 76 Noida residential society towers exterior\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **1. <Entity ref=\"turn892150business12\" value=\"Sethi Max Royal\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 76, Noida\"/>**\n\n      <badge color=\"success\">Metro priority</badge>\n\n      - **Why consider it:** Ready-to-move community near Sector 76 Metro Station.\n      - **For your family:** Central Noida location with daily conveniences nearby.\n      - **Check carefully:** Seepage, basement water issues, parking and the amount of usable green space.\n      - **Budget fit:** Worth checking for resale 3BHKs within your all-in budget.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"JM Orchid Sector 76 Noida residential apartment complex exterior towers\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **2. <Entity ref=\"turn892150business13\" value=\"JM Orchid\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 76, Noida\"/>**\n\n      - **Why consider it:** An occupied society with access to the Sector 76 metro area.\n      - **For your family:** An established community where you can inspect the actual building, amenities and maintenance.\n      - **Check carefully:** Seepage, parking availability, lift condition and registration status.\n      - **Budget fit:** Investigate resale units, particularly flats with older interiors that may be negotiable.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Elite Homz Sector 77 Noida apartment society exterior green landscaped complex\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **3. <Entity ref=\"turn892150business16\" value=\"Elite Homz\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 77, Noida\"/>**\n\n      - **Why consider it:** Ready-to-move apartments in an established residential sector.\n      - **For your family:** A useful balance of central location, residential amenities and access to daily necessities.\n      - **Check carefully:** The exact walking route to metro, apartment condition, maintenance charges and ownership documents.\n      - **Budget fit:** Check whether a suitable 3BHK is available below ₹1.5 crore.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Civitech Sampriti Sector 77 Noida residential society towers landscaped grounds\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **4. <Entity ref=\"turn892150business17\" value=\"Civitech Sampriti\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 77, Noida\"/>**\n\n      - **Why consider it:** Gated society with a range of apartment configurations.\n      - **For your family:** Worth comparing for amenities and usable apartment space.\n      - **Check carefully:** Confirm the specific flat's registry eligibility, title and occupancy documentation before paying a token.\n      - **Budget fit:** Consider only if the actual 3BHK price and additional costs fit your limit.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Nimbus The Hyde Park Sector 78 Noida apartment society landscaped green park towers\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **5. <Entity value=\"Nimbus The Hyde Park\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 78, Noida\"/>**\n\n      - **Why consider it:** An established residential community to compare for open areas and family amenities.\n      - **For your family:** Check the parks, play areas, school routes and the society's maintenance standards.\n      - **Check carefully:** Metro access from the particular tower, apartment condition and total ownership cost.\n      - **Budget fit:** Treat as a price-check candidate; don't assume a 3BHK will fall within budget.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## Listings that currently look closest to your budget\n\nI found two concrete resale leads worth investigating. Prices are advertised asking prices, not independently verified offers.\n\n<box gap={3}>\n  <box border radius=\"xl\" padding={3} gap={2}>\n    <row align=start gap={3}>\n      <AsyncImage query=\"Sethi Max Royal Sector 76 Noida apartment exterior\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"104px\"/>\n      <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n        <title size=\"md\">Sethi Max Royal, Sector 76</title>\n        <title size=\"xl\" color=\"success\">₹1.50 crore</title>\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">3 BHK · 1,125 sq ft · Ready to move</text>\n        A recent advertised listing describes a semi-furnished flat, with two bathrooms and three balconies. However, it lists **no parking**, which is a substantial drawback for a family home. <Cite refs={[\"turn638863search16\"]}/>\n        <text><Link url=\"https://www.nobroker.in/property/buy/3-bhk-apartment-for-sale-in-sector-76-noida/8a9f8a0a6fccd301016fcd3b147a4ccb/detail\" title=\"View listing and contact seller\"/></text>\n      </box>\n    </row>\n    <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">My take: Investigate only if the seller can clarify parking and negotiate the price to leave room for purchase costs.</text>\n  </box>\n  <box border radius=\"xl\" padding={3} gap={2}>\n    <row align=start gap={3}>\n      <AsyncImage query=\"Nimbus The Hyde Park Sector 78 Noida residential towers green landscaped grounds\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"104px\"/>\n      <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n        <title size=\"md\">Nimbus The Hyde Park, Sector 78</title>\n        <title size=\"xl\" color=\"success\">₹1.45 lakh? </title>\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">3 BHK · 1,320 sq ft · Ready to move</text>\n        One listing advertises the flat at **₹1.45 crore**, with three balconies, two bathrooms and one covered plus one open parking space described in the listing. The same page has inconsistent parking information, so confirm directly. <Cite refs={[\"turn638863search6\"]}/>\n        <text><Link url=\"https://housing.com/in/buy/resale/page/19883976-3-bhk-apartment-in-sector-78-for-rs-14500000\" title=\"View listing and contact seller\"/></text>\n      </box>\n    </row>\n    <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">My take: The stronger lead on paper, but the asking price leaves only ₹5 lakh before registration, taxes, brokerage and other purchase costs. Negotiate.</text>\n  </box>\n</box>\n\n**Correction:** The second asking price is ₹1.45 crore, not ₹1.45 lakh.\n\n<MapWidgetV2 points={[{\"id\":\"sethi\",\"name\":\"Sethi Max Royal\",\"address\":\"Sethi Max Royal, Sector 76, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\"},{\"id\":\"jm\",\"name\":\"JM Orchid\",\"address\":\"JM Orchid, Sector 76, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\"},{\"id\":\"hyde\",\"name\":\"Nimbus The Hyde Park\",\"address\":\"Nimbus The Hyde Park, Sector 78, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\"},{\"id\":\"elite\",\"name\":\"Elite Homz\",\"address\":\"Elite Homz, Sector 77, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\"}]} title=\"Shortlisted societies in Central Noida\" />\n\n## What I would do next\n\n1. **Contact the Hyde Park seller first** and confirm whether the flat is still available at ₹1.45 crore, its exact carpet area, parking allocation and final price.\n2. **Check Sethi Max Royal** for a larger or better-priced resale option with confirmed car parking.\n3. Visit both societies during evening peak hours. Check the actual route to Sector 76 Metro Station, commute to your Sector 63 office, nearby schools and the condition of common areas.\n4. Before paying a token, verify ownership, registry eligibility, outstanding dues and the property's approved layout.\n\nOne important point: if ₹1.5 crore is your **total budget including all purchase expenses**, your target advertised price should be lower than ₹1.5 crore.\n\n"
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,
            "weight": 1.0,
            "metadata": {
                "is_merged_message": true,

                    "constants": {
                        "0": "For your budget of ",
                        "1": "₹1.5 crore, I’d start with Sectors 76, 77 and 78 in Central Noida",
                        "2": ", focusing on ready-to-move 3BHK flats near the Aqua Line metro. This keeps you reasonably close to your office in Sector 63 while giving you access to established residential societies, schools, parks and everyday shopping.",
                        "3": "I’d prioritise a well-maintained, already-occupied society over a brand-new project with uncertain possession or registration.",
                        "4": "My shortlist to investigate first",
                        "5": "These are candidate societies, not confirmed available flats. The ₹1.5 crore budget and actual asking prices need to be checked against individual listings.",
                        "6": "a588d0a9-b3f2-5d84-b96a-e7dbc7573701",
                        "7": "1. ",
                        "8": "Sethi Max Royal",
                        "9": "3ffebd24-1b61-5e95-8b90-4667c80853e6",
                        "10": "Metro priority",
                        "11": "Why consider it:",
                        "12": " Ready-to-move community near Sector 76 Metro Station.",
                        "13": "For your family:",
                        "14": " Central Noida location with daily conveniences nearby.",
                        "15": "Check carefully:",
                        "16": " Seepage, basement water issues, parking and the amount of usable green space.",
                        "17": "Budget fit:",
                        "18": " Worth checking for resale 3BHKs within your all-in budget.",
                        "19": "339f3929-b005-5464-9db1-8a1b00da4b7e",
                        "20": "2. ",
                        "21": "JM Orchid",
                        "22": "8b156f63-e995-50c6-9723-932c37fb8cb4",
                        "23": "Why consider it:",
                        "24": " An occupied society with access to the Sector 76 metro area.",
                        "25": "For your family:",
                        "26": " An established community where you can inspect the actual building, amenities and maintenance.",
                        "27": "Check carefully:",
                        "28": " Seepage, parking availability, lift condition and registration status.",
                        "29": "Budget fit:",
                        "30": " Investigate resale units, particularly flats with older interiors that may be negotiable.",
                        "31": "caeda85d-331b-5956-b5d3-e030ea256046",

                        "33": "Elite Homz",
                        "34": "0fc28757-f71e-5bfe-a04f-e3f232ba79fb",
                        "35": "Why consider it:",
                        "36": " Ready-to-move apartments in an established residential sector.",
                        "37": "For your family:",
                        "38": " A useful balance of central location, residential amenities and access to daily necessities.",
                        "39": "Check carefully:",
                        "40": " The exact walking route to metro, apartment condition, maintenance charges and ownership documents.",
                        "41": "Budget fit:",
                        "42": " Check whether a suitable 3BHK is available below ₹1.5 crore.",
                        "43": "6a501449-4b60-543b-91b6-71a191bd9f68",
                        "44": "4. ",

                        "49": "For your family:",

                        "55": "eac36d13-dd19-5522-8e8e-ff2ed1602705",

However, it lists **no parking**, which is a substantial drawback for a family home. citeturn638863search16\n\nurlView listing and contact sellerhttps://www.nobroker.in/property/buy/3-bhk-apartment-for-sale-in-sector-76-noida/8a9f8a0a6fccd301016fcd3b147a4ccb/detail\n\nMy take: Investigate only if the seller can clarify parking and negotiate the price to leave room for purchase costs.\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Nimbus The Hyde Park Sector 78 Noida residential towers green landscaped grounds\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"3:4\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n### Nimbus The Hyde Park, Sector 78\n\n# ₹1.45 lakh?\n\n3 BHK · 1,320 sq ft · Ready to move\n\nOne listing advertises the flat at **₹1.45 crore**, with three balconies, two bathrooms and one covered plus one open parking space described in the listing. The same page has inconsistent parking information, so confirm directly. citeturn638863search6\n\nurlView listing and contact sellerhttps://housing.com/in/buy/resale/page/19883976-3-bhk-apartment-in-sector-78-for-rs-14500000\n\nMy take: The stronger lead on paper, but the asking price leaves only ₹5 lakh before registration, taxes, brokerage and other purchase costs. Negotiate.\n\n**Correction\\:** The second asking price is ₹1.45 crore, not ₹1.45 lakh.\n\ngenui{\"map_widget_v2\":{\"points\":[{\"address\":\"Sethi Max Royal, Sector 76, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\",\"name\":\"Sethi Max Royal\",\"id\":\"sethi\"},{\"address\":\"JM Orchid, Sector 76, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\",\"name\":\"JM Orchid\",\"id\":\"jm\"},{\"address\":\"Nimbus The Hyde Park, Sector 78, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\",\"name\":\"Nimbus The Hyde Park\",\"id\":\"hyde\"},{\"address\":\"Elite Homz, Sector 77, Noida, Uttar Pradesh\",\"name\":\"Elite Homz\",\"id\":\"elite\"}],\"title\":\"Shortlisted societies in Central Noida\"}}\n\n## What I would do next\n\n1. **Contact the Hyde Park seller first** and confirm whether the flat is still available at ₹1.45 crore, its exact carpet area, parking allocation and final price.\n2. **Check Sethi Max Royal** for a larger or better-priced resale option with confirmed car parking.\n3. Visit both societies during evening peak hours. Check the actual route to Sector 76 Metro Station, commute to your Sector 63 office, nearby schools and the condition of common areas.\n4. Before paying a token, verify ownership, registry eligibility, outstanding dues and the property's approved layout.\n\nOne important point: if ₹1.5 crore is your **total budget including all purchase expenses**, your target advertised price should be lower than ₹1.5 crore.",

                                                "text": "⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐\nSethi Royal Max Apartments is one of the well-developed and thoughtfully designed residential societies in Greater Noida. The biggest advantage of this society is its excellent location — it is approximately 500 meters from the metro station, which makes daily commuting very easy for office-goers, students, and families.\nThe society offers a wide range of modern amenities that provide both comfort and convenience. There is a well-maintained swimming pool for relaxation and fitness, along with a fully equipped gym for regular workouts. For spiritual needs, a temple is available inside the premises, creating a peaceful and positive environment.\nFamilies with children will appreciate the dedicated children’s play park, open green parks, and a safe cycling track within the society. Indoor games facilities are also available, making it enjoyable for residents of all age groups.\nParking is properly organized with ample space for residents and visitors. A modern and eco-friendly feature is the availability of charging points for electric cars and bikes, which adds great value in today’s time.\nSecurity is strong and reliable with 24×7 guards and monitoring, ensuring safety and peace of mind for residents. The society maintains cleanliness and has a well-managed environment with good maintenance services.\nOverall, Sethi Royal Max Apartments offers a balanced lifestyle with connectivity, safety, greenery, and modern facilities. It is a great choice for families and professionals looking for a comfortable and premium living experience in Greater Noida.",
                                                "rating": 4.0,
                                                "time_created": "2026-02-17T00:06:17.152000Z"
                                            },
                                            {
                                                "id": null,
                                                "url": null,
                                                "text": "Sethi Max Royal is a peaceful place where middle-class families can live comfortably. The Apartment Owners' Association (AOA) takes care of everything, making sure residents have what they need, like fast repairs and clean surroundings.\n\nWhat makes Sethi Max Royal special is how everyone comes together. They don't just mark special occasions, they celebrate them joyfully, bringing families of different backgrounds closer.\n\nFor middle-class families looking for a welcoming community with great facilities, Sethi Max Royal is the place to be. With the AOA's dedication to making everyone feel at home and providing excellent services, residents can enjoy a happy life full of good times and lasting friendships.",

                                        "enriched_description": "Sethi Max Royal (often referred to as “Max Royal”) is a ready-to-move, gated group-housing apartment complex in Sector 76, Noida, developed by Sethi Group. The community is positioned as a mid-to-premium family society with a strong emphasis on day-to-day convenience (metro access and nearby markets), on-site amenities (clubhouse, pool, gym, indoor games), and a generally calm, residential environment.\n\nLocation & connectivity\nA recurring theme across resident feedback is connectivity—especially proximity to Sector 76 Metro Station. Multiple reviews describe it as walkable and convenient for commuting; one detailed resident review notes it is “approximately 500 meters from the metro station,” while other residents frame it as “within 1 km” or “very near.” The society sits on/near a main road, which is a double-edged sword: it improves access to transit and nearby shops, but some residents mention “traffic chaos” outside and concerns about children’s safety because vehicles move close to the boundary.\n\nResidential profile (homes & layout)\nListings and resident commentary commonly describe the project as comprising primarily 2 BHK and 3 BHK apartments (with some 2.5 BHK configurations mentioned on property portals). Residents frequently characterize the society as suitable for “middle-class families,” working professionals, and senior citizens, emphasizing a “peaceful” and “home-like” environment rather than a purely investment-driven or transient rental vibe.\n\nAmenities & facilities (what residents highlight)\nThe strongest positive feedback centers on the amenity mix and how usable it feels for daily life. Commonly mentioned highlights include:\n- Swimming pool and gym: repeatedly cited as key lifestyle amenities (“park for kids, gym n swimming pool as well,” and “fully equipped gym” / “well-maintained swimming pool”).\n- Clubhouse and indoor recreation: residents and property sources reference indoor games and spaces for activities; one resident review explicitly mentions indoor games “making it enjoyable for residents of all age groups.”\n- Kid-friendly features: small parks, kid zones, and play areas are frequently mentioned. One review appreciates “dedicated children’s play park, open green parks, and a safe cycling track.”\n- Jogging/cycling track: called out in multiple places as part of the society’s active-living infrastructure.\n- Temple: several reviews note a “small temple” inside the premises, valued for spiritual needs and a “peaceful and positive environment.”\n- Convenience retail: residents mention a “small market almost inside the society” for daily needs, plus nearby shops and food options outside the complex.\n- Parking: generally described as organized, with “ample space” noted by some residents; however, parking adequacy is not uniformly praised (see lowlights).\n- EV charging: at least one detailed review mentions charging points for electric cars and bikes, described as a modern, eco-friendly plus.\n\nSecurity, maintenance & management\nSecurity is a consistent strength in reviews: “24/7 security,” guards, CCTV surveillance, and a “safe environment” are repeatedly highlighted. Several residents also attribute improvements in upkeep to the Apartment Owners’ Association (AOA) or a change in management. One longer resident review states that the AOA “takes care of everything,” citing “fast repairs and clean surroundings,” while another (from an owner review on a housing portal) notes “positive changes,” including that “maintenance has improved” and that “security has notably improved since management changed.”\n\nCleanliness and daily operations also come up positively: residents describe the society as “peaceful, clean, and well-maintained,” and one review specifically praises housekeeping (“Housekeeping staff really good.”).\n\nCommunity & social life\nBeyond infrastructure, residents often emphasize community cohesion. A recurring theme is that festivals and events are celebrated together, helping neighbors bond. One review highlights that residents “celebrate them joyfully, bringing families of different backgrounds closer,” and another notes the society feels like “a home, not just a place to live.” This suggests a relatively engaged resident base and active community life.\n\nCommon complaints / lowlights (balanced view)\nWhile the overall sentiment is positive, reviews surface several recurring drawbacks:\n- Maintenance cost/value concerns: some residents feel charges are “very high” compared with nearby societies, and at least one housing-portal review calls maintenance “a bit costly as per market standard.”\n- Green space size: multiple comments suggest parks/garden areas are smaller than ideal. Examples include “Park area is very small for children,” and feedback that “greenery is a little less” or the “garden is bit small.”\n- Traffic and child safety outside: because the complex is near a main road, some residents mention heavy traffic and say it “wasn’t safe for young kids” due to constant vehicle movement.\n- Water/seepage during rains: a recurring concern in older and mixed reviews is seepage/leakage in rainy seasons and water-logging/basement flooding. Some residents report these issues historically, while others say conditions improved after maintenance changes (“addressing issues like seepage and plaster falling” and now “no seepage issues”).\n- Occasional quality/repair issues: a minority of reviews complain about construction/plaster/painting quality, though others praise the building as “strong” and note repainting and repairs.\n\nPractical tips for prospective residents/visitors\n- Best fit: families seeking metro proximity, a gated environment, and a socially active society with core amenities (pool/gym/clubhouse) tend to rate it highly.\n- Considerations: if you prioritize large green areas, quiet frontage away from traffic, or very low monthly outgoings, you may want to compare with nearby societies in Sector 76/77/78.\n- Rainy season due diligence: ask current residents/management about recent drainage, basement water-logging controls, and seepage remediation status.\n- Daily needs: residents report convenient access to small markets/shops nearby and within/adjacent to the society, which can reduce reliance on longer errands.\n\nOverall, Sethi Max Royal is widely perceived as a well-located Sector 76 residential society with strong metro connectivity, a broad amenity set, and a generally safe, community-oriented environment—tempered by recurring concerns around traffic at the frontage, relatively limited green/play areas for some families, and periodic (or historical) monsoon-related seepage/water-logging issues.",

                                                "url": null,
                                                "text": "It's a well maintained society with 8 wings in total , each with G+ 13 floors .There is underground/ Basement parking facility available. There are two 3 gates to the society, two back gates and a front one. 24*7 security guards are posted at each gates and at the entrance of the each wing too. Ample and clean walking space, playground area for kids with enough swings and adequate plantation within the campus add to the advantages of the place. There is a market area on the back side of the society for every essential commodities including salon and eateries too.The flats available in the society are 2 and 3 BHK.",

                                            {
                                                "id": null,
                                                "url": null,

                                                "url": null,

                                        "service_providers": null,
                                        "enriched_description": "JM Orchid is a gated residential apartment complex in Sector 76, Noida, developed by JM Housing. It is widely described across property portals as a completed, ready-to-move society spread over ~5 acres with about 9 residential towers and roughly 678 apartments (some residents informally describe “wings/blocks,” which can lead to slight variation in tower counts). Homes are primarily 2 and 3 BHK configurations (with some listings also mentioning studio/“2.5/3.5” style layouts), and the society is positioned as a mid-to-upper mid segment community with a strong emphasis on day-to-day convenience and metro connectivity.\n\nLocation & connectivity\nA major recurring advantage mentioned by residents and portals is proximity to the Noida Sector 76 Metro area and overall connectivity within Central Noida. Residents commonly highlight that autos/cabs are easy to find and that the metro is close enough to be practical for regular commuting. The surrounding neighborhood is also described as having growing retail and services nearby, which reduces dependency on long drives for essentials.\n\nOn-campus conveniences (shopping plaza/market)\nA distinctive practical feature is the presence of a market/shopping-plaza style strip associated with the society (often referred to as “JM Orchid Market” or similar). Multiple businesses list themselves at JM Orchid with shop numbers, including a pharmacy and laundry/dry-clean services, reinforcing the idea that residents can access daily needs within or immediately adjacent to the complex. In older resident feedback, this is echoed as “a market area on the back side of the society for essential commodities including salon and eateries.”\n\nBuildings, layout & apartment feel\nResident feedback is generally positive on cleanliness and upkeep of common areas, with several comments pointing to “clean walking space,” plantation/greenery, and a sense of safety. One detailed review describes the society as “well maintained,” with basement/underground parking and multiple entry gates, plus 24×7 guards at gates and near tower entrances.\n\nApartment interiors are typically characterized as spacious and well-ventilated, with good natural light. Property portals list standard residential finishes such as vitrified tiles in living/dining and bedrooms (with some units described as having laminated/wooden flooring in the master), ceramic tiles in kitchen/toilets/balconies, concealed copper wiring, and common modern fittings (CP fittings, provision for geyser, granite kitchen platform with stainless-steel sink, aluminium sliding windows, etc.).\n\nAmenities & facilities (what residents mention most)\nAmenities are a major part of JM Orchid’s appeal. Across resident reviews and listings, the most frequently referenced facilities include:\n- Swimming pool\n- Gym/fitness center\n- Clubhouse/community areas\n- Children’s play area and parks/green zones\n- Indoor games (mentioned by some residents)\n- Sports courts/areas (basketball/badminton are commonly listed)\n- Jogging/walking paths and landscaped gardens\n\nResidents who rate the society highly often summarize it as having “all amenities… available and well-maintained,” and some call out an active social environment where neighbors are cooperative and festivals are celebrated.\n\nSecurity, maintenance & day-to-day operations\nSecurity is a consistent positive theme. Reviews frequently describe the society as “safe,” “secure,” and well-guarded. Maintenance is also often rated well in terms of cleanliness and responsiveness; one review highlight states: “Maintenance and service requests are handled quickly and efficiently.”\n\nThat said, residents also flag that operational choices (notably power backup) can influence monthly costs. One resident review notes that “multiple power backup facilities… contribute to fluctuating maintenance charges,” and that the cost of power backup can raise fees.\n\nCommon complaints / lowlights (recurring issues)\nFeedback is not uniformly positive, and several issues recur across resident commentary:\n- Seepage and water-related problems: Multiple residents mention seepage issues, and some specifically call out basement flooding/waterlogging during the rainy season/monsoons. One resident review states seepage is “widespread,” and that the basement “tends to flood during the rainy season.”\n- Parking constraints: While covered/basement parking is a plus, some residents still feel the “parking area size” needs improvement (often interpreted as tight spaces, visitor parking constraints, or peak-time congestion).\n- Clubhouse/kids facilities: Some residents feel the clubhouse is “inadequate” or lacks enough features—particularly for children.\n- Policy/community fit: At least one resident review explicitly mentions “bachelors are not permitted,” which can be important for renters, students, or shared-housing arrangements.\n- Connectivity quirks: A smaller set of reviews mention issues like buses being less readily available nearby and occasional mobile network problems.\n\nWho it tends to suit best\nBased on the overall pattern of reviews, JM Orchid is best suited for families and working professionals who value a gated community with on-site amenities (pool/gym/play areas), a generally clean and secure environment, and easy access to metro commuting. It may be less suitable for those who are highly sensitive to monsoon water/seepage risks (buyers/renters should inspect basements and common areas during/after rains if possible), or for renters seeking a bachelor-friendly society.\n\nPractical tips before buying/renting\n- Ask specifically about seepage history in the tower/stack you’re considering, and how monsoon waterlogging is managed.\n- Clarify maintenance charges and what power backup covers (common areas only vs. in-apartment backup), as residents note this can affect monthly costs.\n- Confirm parking allocation (owner vs visitor) and any rules for additional vehicles.\n- If renting, verify society policies (including any restrictions on bachelors/tenants) upfront.\n\nOverall, JM Orchid is perceived as a well-located, amenity-rich residential society in Sector 76 with strong security and generally positive cleanliness/maintenance feedback, balanced by recurring concerns around seepage/basement water issues, parking constraints, and cost variability tied to power backup and maintenance.",
                                        "from_cache": false
                                    },

                                        "enriched_description": "## Overview\nIITL Nimbus The Hyde Park is a large, gated high-rise residential township in Sector 78, Noida. The developer’s project information describes it as a ~15-acre development with a high proportion of open/landscaped area (marketed as 82% open spaces) and a mix of apartment formats ranging from studio/1 BHK up to 4 BHK. The project is built as a multi-tower community (23 towers, with towers marketed as up to 23 floors) with a large number of homes (about 2,000+ units) and has been handed over with an RWA (Resident Welfare Association) in place.\n\nAcross resident feedback, the most consistent positives are the “society design,” open green spaces between towers, and the sense of a self-contained community with amenities. Typical comments include “Plenty of open space and greenery between the towers is really eye catching” and “The way the society is designed is very good. Central park is good.” Residents also frequently mention proximity to metro connectivity and the broader Sector 78/76 area as a convenience.\n\n## Layout, open space, and community feel\nA recurring theme is the campus-like layout: multiple towers with internal parks and walking areas, plus a central park/green that becomes the focal point for gatherings. Reviewers highlight the greenery and open areas as a key differentiator versus denser societies.\n\nThe social environment is often described as warm and community-oriented, with multiple residents calling out festival celebrations as a highlight: “Festivals are celebrated very nicely especially Durga pooja, Holi, Christmas, 15 august, 26 January and all… Very warm people. Safe society.”\n\n## Amenities and facilities (what’s commonly mentioned)\nThe Hyde Park is positioned as an amenity-rich complex. The developer’s amenity list includes:\n- Clubhouse with leisure and event facilities (including banquet/event capacity), gym, swimming pool, kids’ play areas, and indoor games.\n- Outdoor sports and recreation such as jogging track, badminton/basketball/multipurpose courts, skating rink, landscaped gardens, amphitheatre, meditation/yoga areas, and other outdoor features.\n- Convenience and security infrastructure such as intercom, CCTV/monitoring, power backup, fire-fighting systems, and parking.\n\nFrom residents, the swimming pool is a standout. One reviewer notes: “One thing which I really liked was the swimming pool which is really good. Most societies have a pool just for the namesake.” Others echo the presence of a gym, clubhouse, parks, and kids’ play areas as everyday-use amenities.\n\n## Location, connectivity, and nearby conveniences\nLocation is repeatedly described as “central Noida” / “superb location,” with residents pointing to proximity to schools, malls, hospitals, and markets: “Everything is near school, mall, hospital, markets everything is in close proximity.” Several reviews mention that the project is near an (existing/upcoming) metro station and that connectivity to Delhi is generally good.\n\n## Construction quality and apartment sizing (balanced view)\nConstruction quality is the most common concern in resident feedback.\n- Some residents are positive or neutral (“Not sure about the construction quality…”) while others are explicit: “the internal construction material of flats is poor,” “some flat quality is average,” and “Size is bit small but ok.”\n- On broader review platforms, common negatives include seepage/waterproofing issues and periodic maintenance-related complaints. Examples include mentions of “seepage issue” and concerns that “there might have been underlying issues with the construction quality.” A few reviews go further into structural anxiety (e.g., reports of slab/plaster issues), though these are individual reports rather than universal experiences.\n\nOverall, the signal suggests: the campus and amenity experience is often rated higher than the finishing/quality consistency inside some flats, and prospective buyers/tenants commonly evaluate tower/stack/flat condition carefully rather than assuming uniform quality across all units.\n\n## Maintenance, utilities, and day-to-day operations\nDay-to-day operations receive mixed feedback:\n- Positives include timely garbage collection and a generally “clean & well maintained” feel in some reviews, along with power backup being appreciated.\n- Negatives include complaints about cleaning in certain areas (“minimal cleaning… pathways”), lift cleanliness/uptime (“occasional operational problems”), and occasional utility reliability issues. Some reviews cite power cuts/voltage fluctuations and intermittent water/electricity disruptions, while others report “low power cuts” and a smooth experience—suggesting variability by time period, tower, or personal tolerance.\n\n## Security, safety, and rules\nSecurity is frequently mentioned as a strength: residents reference guards/CCTV and a “safe society” feeling. At the same time, like many high-rise communities, residents emphasize the importance of balcony/child safety and adherence to society rules, especially during crowded festival events.\n\n## Atmosphere, noise, and events\nThe complex is often described as lively due to frequent celebrations and events. This is a positive for residents who enjoy community activities, but it can be a downside for those sensitive to noise. One review notes people “have problem with sound,” and another mentions event stalls/food being expensive during a mela (“very costly food stuff”).\n\n## Who it tends to suit best\n- Families who prioritize a gated campus with parks, kids’ play areas, and regular community events.\n- Residents who value amenities (pool, gym, clubhouse) and want day-to-day conveniences nearby.\n- Commuters who want metro access and connectivity to major Noida/Delhi routes.\n\nIt may be less ideal for:\n- Buyers/tenants who want consistently premium interior finishes across all units without needing to shortlist specific towers/stacks.\n- Residents who strongly prefer quiet, low-activity communities.\n\n## Practical tips before you move\n- If renting/buying, inspect for seepage, lift condition, corridor/basement cleanliness, and power backup performance in the specific tower.\n- Ask current residents about recent maintenance responsiveness and any ongoing waterproofing/utility issues.\n- If you’re noise-sensitive, check proximity to the central park/event lawn and clubhouse areas.\n\n(Phone numbers found online for this location may route to sales/enquiry rather than the RWA/maintenance office; residents typically rely on society channels/maintenance desk for day-to-day support.)",
                                        "from_cache": false

                                        "enriched_description": "Elite Homz is a large, ready-to-move residential condominium/apartment society in Sector 77, Central Noida, developed by Elite Group (often listed in property portals as “HR Oracle Elite Homz”). The project is positioned as a “premium society” and is frequently described by residents and visitors as well-maintained, amenity-rich, and family-friendly. It’s a multi-tower gated community (commonly cited as 8 towers) with a sizeable number of apartments (commonly cited around 572 units) and on-site retail/shops.\n\nResidential offering & layout\nElite Homz is primarily known for 2 BHK, 3 BHK, and 4 BHK apartments. The developer markets the community as having an eco-friendly, Vastu-oriented layout, with a large proportion of green/open area and “corner flat” planning. Property listings and project summaries commonly reference practical in-home utilities such as piped gas, RO water system, intercom, and power backup support.\n\nAmenities & facilities\nA key draw is the breadth of “society amenities” typically expected from a premium gated complex in Noida. Across project summaries and listings, the amenity set commonly includes: a club house/community spaces, gym/fitness facilities (including an aerobics room), swimming pool, jogging/strolling tracks, landscaped gardens/open lawns, indoor games areas, and multiple sports/recreation options (often listed as badminton/tennis-style courts and other outdoor games areas). Safety and convenience features frequently associated with the complex include gated entry, multi-layer security/CCTV references, lifts, reserved/visitor parking, and power backup.\n\nOn-site conveniences (market/shops/cafes)\nOne of the most repeated resident/visitor themes is the convenience of having day-to-day shopping and casual food options nearby or within the immediate complex ecosystem. Reviews mention “a very good market” where you can find “medicine shops, eateries, fruits, vegetable, groceries,” plus “hang out kiosks.” Another review highlight describes it as a “nice complex with lots of small open restaurant that offers good food in budget.” This combination—housing plus daily-need retail and casual dining—appears to be a major reason people see the society as comfortable for routine living.\n\nLocation, connectivity & nearby essentials\nElite Homz sits in Sector 77, which residents often frame as “central Noida.” Reviewers point out the advantage of nearby “top schools and multispeciality hospitals,” and the general presence of other “premium societies” in the surrounding sectors. For working professionals, one review specifically calls out shorter commute times for those working toward the Sector 62 industrial/corporate area. As with many Sector 7x Noida societies, the value proposition is a balance of residential calm with access to everyday infrastructure and major employment corridors.\n\nGeneral perception from resident/visitor feedback\nOverall sentiment trends positive and is strongly anchored in maintenance quality and the “premium” feel. Short, emphatic reviews describe the society as “Beautiful lovely amazing marvellous” and repeatedly call it “very good and maintained” / “well maintained society with all amenities.” Another recurring theme is a kid-friendly environment—one highlighted review states: “I loved the kid friendly environment there the most.”\n\nService, staff & management (as reflected in reviews)\nWhile detailed service narratives are limited in the provided review set, there are positive mentions of people interactions on the premises. One highlight states “Very good place and kind staff,” suggesting that security/maintenance/onsite staff interactions are generally perceived as courteous.\n\nPractical notes for prospective residents/visitors\n• Best fit: People looking for a premium, amenity-forward gated society in Central Noida—especially families who value kid-friendly spaces and having daily-needs shopping close by.\n• Lifestyle convenience: Strong emphasis on nearby/on-site markets, kiosks, and casual eateries, plus proximity to schools and hospitals.\n• Commute considerations: Often viewed as convenient for those traveling toward established office/industrial areas (e.g., Sector 62).\n• What to verify before renting/buying: Tower-specific maintenance standards, power-backup coverage in your unit, parking allocation (covered vs open), and how close your building is to the market/retail strip (noise/footfall can vary by block).\n\nIn short, Elite Homz is widely regarded as a well-kept, premium residential society in Sector 77, Noida, with a strong amenity package and notable everyday convenience due to nearby/on-site retail and budget-friendly food options—features that show up repeatedly in resident and visitor commentary.",

                "text": "{\"query\":\"The user is shopping for a 3BHK flat in Noida, previously indicated a family home near Sector 63 office with good schools, open space, newer society, and metro connectivity.             "id": "d7910081-9d17-470b-9f6e-e12e048a071f",
            "author": {

            "create_time": 1792072279.955038,
            "update_time": 1792072295.651142,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "That changes the shortlist. I’d use **₹1.8 crore as a ceiling, not a target**, and pay extra only for a demonstrably better-built apartment, reliable maintenance and a better family living environment.\n\nGiven your office in Sector 63, family needs, metro preference and preference for Noida proper, I’d concentrate on Sectors 75–78. But there’s an important distinction: a premium-looking tower or expensive clubhouse is not proof of better construction.\n\n## The societies I’d investigate now\n\n<box gap={4}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Mahagun Moderne Sector 78 Noida residential towers landscaped green campus\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **1. <Entity ref=\"turn643455business4\" value=\"Mahagun Moderne\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 78, Noida\"/>**\n\n      <badge color=\"success\">First inspection</badge>\n\n      A useful benchmark for a family-focused, established society with greenery and convenient access to Central Noida. Resident feedback praises maintenance and connectivity, but also mentions seepage, plaster deterioration and waterlogging in some areas. <Cite refs={[\"turn643455search0\",\"turn643455search1\"]}/>\n\n      **My view:** Inspect individual flats and common areas before deciding. Don't pay a premium just for the society's reputation.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Mahagun Mezzaria Sector 78 Noida luxury residential high rise building exterior\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **2. <Entity ref=\"turn643455business2\" value=\"Mahagun Mezzaria\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 78, Noida\"/>**\n\n      A higher-end comparison candidate in the same general area. I'd investigate its apartment specifications, structural and waterproofing history, maintenance records and resale prices.\n\n      **My view:** Consider it only if an actual 3BHK fits your price ceiling after additional charges. Its premium positioning alone does not establish superior construction.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Civitech Sampriti Sector 77 Noida apartment towers entrance landscaped campus\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **3. <Entity ref=\"turn643455business3\" value=\"Civitech Sampriti\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 77, Noida\"/>**\n\n      Worth comparing for apartment layouts and everyday practicality in Sector 77.\n\n      **My view:** Make this a value comparison against the more premium options. Inspect bathrooms, external walls, balconies, window sealing, plumbing and basement condition rather than judging from the entrance.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Prateek Wisteria Sector 77 Noida apartment society towers landscaped open green spaces\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **4. <Entity ref=\"turn643455business5\" value=\"Prateek Wisteria\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 77, Noida\"/>**\n\n      A useful alternative for comparing the residential environment, green spaces and upkeep in Sector 77.\n\n      **My view:** Shortlist a specific resale flat only after speaking with residents in its tower and checking the building's maintenance history.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n<MapWidgetV2 title=\"Societies to compare in Central Noida\" points={[{\"ref\":\"turn643455business4\"},{\"ref\":\"turn643455business2\"},{\"ref\":\"turn643455business3\"},{\"ref\":\"turn643455business5\"}]} carousel={{\"visibility\":\"hidden\"}}/>\n\n## What I would avoid paying extra for\n\nI would not automatically upgrade to Nimbus The Hyde Park simply because a seller offers a larger flat or a better-looking campus. A consumer commission judgment dated November 30, 2023, documented construction defects and delayed completion in a specific apartment dispute involving the project. That doesn't prove every flat has defects, but it is a reason to investigate carefully. <Cite refs={[\"turn643455search6\",\"turn643455search2\"]}/>\n\nLikewise, Sethi Max Royal remains a metro-oriented option, but reports of seepage and rain-related waterlogging mean it should pass the same inspection standard as every other candidate. <Cite refs={[\"turn643455search4\",\"turn643455search5\"]}/>\n\n## My construction-quality inspection checklist\n\nWhen you visit a shortlisted flat, check these five things.\n\n- **Waterproofing:** Look for fresh paint patches, damp corners, bubbling paint, bathroom leakage and seepage around windows.\n- **Concrete and plaster:** Check for recurring cracks, exposed reinforcement, crumbling plaster and signs of repairs in the basement and common areas. Have a qualified structural engineer assess concerning cracks.\n- **Plumbing and drainage:** Test water pressure, bathroom drainage, balcony outlets and signs of leakage below wet areas.\n- **Building maintenance:** Inspect the basement after rain if possible, the lift condition, fire systems, water supply and the maintenance team's repair records.\n- **Independent evidence:** Speak to at least three residents in different apartments and ask whether they have had recurring structural, leakage or plumbing problems.\n\nFor an older resale flat, actual performance over several monsoons can tell you more than a brochure promising premium construction.\n\n## How I would use your ₹1.8 crore budget\n\n| Purchase scenario | My approach |\n|---|---|\n| ₹1.5–1.6 crore | Preferred range if the flat and society pass inspection |\n| ₹1.6–1.7 crore | Worth paying for better condition, layout or maintenance |\n| ₹1.7–1.8 crore | Only if there is clear evidence of better quality and the total cost remains within budget |\n\nThese are buying thresholds, not verified market prices for the societies above. Registration, stamp duty, brokerage, repairs and other charges can push the total beyond the advertised price.\n\n**My recommendation:** Start with Mahagun Moderne as a comparison benchmark, inspect Civitech Sampriti and Prateek Wisteria alongside it, and consider Mahagun Mezzaria if a suitable resale flat is financially feasible. I haven't verified a currently available 3BHK in these societies at a particular price, so the next step is to match the inspection shortlist against live resale inventory.\n\nOne question that will materially change the shortlist: **Would you accept a 7–12-year-old society if its construction and maintenance are demonstrably better, or do you want a society no more than five years old?**"
                ]
            },

That doesn't prove every flat has defects, but it is a reason to investigate carefully. citeturn643455search6turn643455search2\n\nLikewise, Sethi Max Royal remains a metro-oriented option, but reports of seepage and rain-related waterlogging mean it should pass the same inspection standard as every other candidate. citeturn643455search4turn643455search5\n\n## My construction-quality inspection checklist\n\nWhen you visit a shortlisted flat, check these five things.\n\n- **Waterproofing\\:** Look for fresh paint patches, damp corners, bubbling paint, bathroom leakage and seepage around windows.\n- **Concrete and plaster\\:** Check for recurring cracks, exposed reinforcement, crumbling plaster and signs of repairs in the basement and common areas. Have a qualified structural engineer assess concerning cracks.\n- **Plumbing and drainage\\:** Test water pressure, bathroom drainage, balcony outlets and signs of leakage below wet areas.\n- **Building maintenance\\:** Inspect the basement after rain if possible, the lift condition, fire systems, water supply and the maintenance team's repair records.\n- **Independent evidence\\:** Speak to at least three residents in different apartments and ask whether they have had recurring structural, leakage or plumbing problems.\n\nFor an older resale flat, actual performance over several monsoons can tell you more than a brochure promising premium construction.\n\n## How I would use your ₹1.8 crore budget\n\n| Purchase scenario | My approach |\n|--------|--------|\n| ₹1.5–1.6 crore | Preferred range if the flat and society pass inspection |\n| ₹1.6–1.7 crore | Worth paying for better condition, layout or maintenance |\n| ₹1.7–1.8 crore | Only if there is clear evidence of better quality and the total cost remains within budget |\n\nThese are buying thresholds, not verified market prices for the societies above. Registration, stamp duty, brokerage, repairs and other charges can push the total beyond the advertised price.\n\n**My recommendation\\:** Start with Mahagun Moderne as a comparison benchmark, inspect Civitech Sampriti and Prateek Wisteria alongside it, and consider Mahagun Mezzaria if a suitable resale flat is financially feasible. I haven't verified a currently available 3BHK in these societies at a particular price, so the next step is to match the inspection shortlist against live resale inventory.\n\nOne question that will materially change the shortlist: **Would you accept a 7–12-year-old society if its construction and maintenance are demonstrably better, or do you want a society no more than five years old?**",

                                "url": "https://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/city/noida/no-proper-mkts-parks-what-ails-new-sectors-even-after-a-decade/articleshow/123664085.cms",
                                "title": "No proper mkts, parks: What ails 'new sectors' even after a decade",
                                "snippet": "Over a decade after residents began occupying apartments in Noida’s 7x (Sectors 74–79) and 100x (Sectors 115–121) sectors, basic infrastructure remains severely lacking. These areas—home to around 3 lakh people—suffer from absence of proper roads, local transport, parks, commercial markets, petrol pumps, banks, and health facilities. Despite improvements like e-rickshaws operating from nearby metro stations and internal builder-led developments, essential public amenities are still undeveloped. There are only two dairy booths for many high-rises, and the nearest petrol pump is 7 km away. A proposed market in Sector 76 remains unbuilt, and major connecting roads are broken, leading to daily traffic congestion and dust pollution. Waste mismanagement is a major issue, with garbage being dumped in green spaces. Sector 117 residents have protested against a designated park area being used for waste dumping, while lack of water supply backup, security features, and public facilities continues to impact quality of life. While the Noida Authority has taken minor steps like earmarking vending areas, major infrastructural development is still pending, prompting demands and frustrations from residents.",

                                "title": "Noida residents oppose common area maintenance fee hike",
                                "snippet": "Residents of the Mahagun Moderne Society in Sector 7,8, Noida, held a protest on Sunday opposing a recent hike in the Common Area Maintenance (CAM) charges imposed by the Apartment Owners Association (AOA). The demonstrators expressed their dissatisfaction with the increased fees, which they believe to be unjustified. The protest highlights growing tensions between residents and society management regarding maintenance costs and transparency in financial decisions.",

        {
            "id": "757aa0f1-3dfb-4cae-a6c7-66564436a04b",
            "author": {
                "role": "user",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1792072297.46,
            "update_time": 1792072310.706405,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "“I found a resale flat for ₹1.65 crore. Is it a better deal than a new project at ₹1.8 crore?”"
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": null,
            "weight": 1.0,
            "metadata": {
                "request_id": "bdc7ab2c-3dce-4fa2-a7ff-15fc84d5d179",
                "message_source": null,
                "turn_exchange_id": "5df4349d-f81a-4cd2-8001-112036c12458",
                "working_turn_id": "5df4349d-f81a-4cd2-8001-112036c12458",
                "can_save": false
            },
            "recipient": "all",
            "channel": null
        },
        {
            "id": "179513e4-f355-4e67-b0d6-4413905a6864",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1792072300.495793,
            "update_time": 1792072309.744163,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "**The ₹1.65 crore resale flat could be the better deal, especially given that construction quality matters more to you than a clubhouse.** But I wouldn't choose it over the ₹1.8 crore new project on price alone. The society, actual construction condition, legal status and all-in cost matter more than the ₹15 lakh difference.\n\nHere's how I'd compare them for your family in Noida.\n\n## 1. Resale vs new project\n\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={3} gap={3}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"44px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"building-2\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">Option A: Resale flat</title>\n      <title size=\"2xl\">₹1.65 crore</title>\n      <badge color=\"success\">My provisional preference</badge>\n      - You can inspect the actual apartment, finishes, plumbing and common areas.\n      - You can speak to residents about leakage, maintenance and construction defects.\n      - You may be able to move in sooner, subject to vacant possession and paperwork.\n      - Older interiors or upcoming repairs could reduce the apparent savings.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"44px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"construction\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">Option B: New project</title>\n      <title size=\"2xl\">₹1.8 crore</title>\n      <badge>Conditional alternative</badge>\n      - Newer fixtures and finishes, potentially lower initial repair costs.\n      - A better layout or building specification may justify the premium.\n      - If under construction, you take possession-delay and construction-quality risks.\n      - The quoted price may exclude parking, GST where applicable, registration, floor premiums or other charges.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## 2. Is the ₹15 lakh saving real?\n\nThe price difference is 8.3% of the new project's quoted price. But compare the final cost of owning each property, not just the advertised price.\n\n| Cost component | Resale | New project |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| Quoted price | ₹1.65 Cr | ₹1.80 Cr |\n| Registration and taxes | Verify | Verify |\n| Brokerage and transfer charges | Verify | Verify |\n| Repairs and interiors | Estimate after inspection | Estimate from specifications |\n| Parking and other charges | Verify | Verify |\n| **Actual all-in cost** | Calculate | Calculate |\n\nFor example, if the resale needs ₹5 lakh of repairs and the new project has ₹5 lakh of additional charges that the resale doesn't have, the effective gap is still around ₹15 lakh before accounting for other differences. These are illustrative amounts, not estimates for either property.\n\n## 3. What would make me choose each one?\n\nChoose the **₹1.65 crore resale** if:\n- The society has a good record of maintenance and few recurring construction defects.\n- The flat has no material seepage, structural or plumbing problems.\n- Ownership, registry eligibility and outstanding dues check out.\n- It has usable open space, good metro access and a practical commute to Sector 63.\n- Its remaining life-cycle costs are reasonable.\n\nChoose the **₹1.8 crore new project** if:\n- The builder and project have a stronger verified delivery and quality record.\n- The specifications and independent inspection evidence justify the premium.\n- The project is ready, or you can comfortably manage the possession timeline.\n- The total cost remains within your budget.\n\n## 4. Give me these details and I'll compare the two properly\n\nI need to know which two properties you're comparing before I can make a firm recommendation.\n\n{@body const [resale,setResale] = DIL.useState(\"\")}\n{@body const [newProject,setNewProject] = DIL.useState(\"\")}\n{@body const [status,setStatus] = DIL.useState(\"Ready to move\")}\n{@body const [area,setArea] = DIL.useState(\"Similar carpet area\")}\n{@body const [age,setAge] = DIL.useState(\"7–12 years\")}\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={4} gap={4}>\n  <box gap={1}>\n    <label>Resale society and sector</label>\n    <input value={resale} onChange={setResale} placeholder=\"e.g. Mahagun Moderne, Sector 78\"/>\n  </box>\n  <box gap={1}>\n    <label>New project and sector</label>\n    <input value={newProject} onChange={setNewProject} placeholder=\"e.g. project name, Sector 150\"/>\n  </box>\n  <box gap={2}>\n    <label>New project's current status</label>\n    <radio-group value={status} onChange={setStatus} direction=\"col\">\n      <radio value=\"Ready to move\">Ready to move</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Near completion\">Near completion</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Under construction\">Under construction</radio>\n    </radio-group>\n  </box>\n  <box gap={2}>\n    <label>Are the flat sizes comparable?</label>\n    <radio-group value={area} onChange={setArea} direction=\"col\">\n      <radio value=\"Similar carpet area\">Similar carpet area</radio>\n      <radio value=\"New project is larger\">New project is larger</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Resale is larger\">Resale is larger</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Not sure\">Not sure</radio>\n    </radio-group>\n  </box>\n  <box gap={2}>\n    <label>Approximate age of the resale society</label>\n    <radio-group value={age} onChange={setAge} direction=\"col\">\n      <radio value=\"Under 5 years\">Under 5 years</radio>\n      <radio value=\"5–7 years\">5–7 years</radio>\n      <radio value=\"7–12 years\">7–12 years</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Over 12 years\">Over 12 years</radio>\n      <radio value=\"Not sure\">Not sure</radio>\n    </radio-group>\n  </box>\n  <button block onClick={() => GenUI.issueNewTurn(\"Compare these Noida property options for a family purchase, prioritising verified construction quality, maintenance, metro connectivity and all-in cost. Resale: ₹1.65 crore, society/sector: \"+(resale.trim()||\"not provided\")+\". New project: ₹1.8 crore, project/sector: \"+(newProject.trim()||\"not provided\")+\". New project status: \"+status+\". Size comparison: \"+area+\". Resale society age: \"+age+\". Identify missing information and assess risks; do not assume unverified prices or availability.\")}>Compare my two options</button>\n</box>\n\nWith those details, I can assess whether the new project genuinely justifies the extra ₹15 lakh or whether the resale offers better value and lower risk."
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,
            "weight": 1.0,

Is the ₹15 lakh saving real?\n\nThe price difference is 8.3% of the new project's quoted price. But compare the final cost of owning each property, not just the advertised price.\n\n| Cost component | Resale | New project |\n|--------|--------|--------|\n| Quoted price | ₹1.65 Cr | ₹1.80 Cr |\n| Registration and taxes | Verify | Verify |\n| Brokerage and transfer charges | Verify | Verify |\n| Repairs and interiors | Estimate after inspection | Estimate from specifications |\n| Parking and other charges | Verify | Verify |\n| **Actual all-in cost** | Calculate | Calculate |\n\nFor example, if the resale needs ₹5 lakh of repairs and the new project has ₹5 lakh of additional charges that the resale doesn't have, the effective gap is still around ₹15 lakh before accounting for other differences. These are illustrative amounts, not estimates for either property.\n\n## 3\\. What would make me choose each one?\n\nChoose the **₹1.65 crore resale** if\\:\n\n- The society has a good record of maintenance and few recurring construction defects.\n- The flat has no material seepage, structural or plumbing problems.\n- Ownership, registry eligibility and outstanding dues check out.\n- It has usable open space, good metro access and a practical commute to Sector 63.\n- Its remaining life-cycle costs are reasonable.\n\nChoose the **₹1.8 crore new project** if\\:\n\n- The builder and project have a stronger verified delivery and quality record.\n- The specifications and independent inspection evidence justify the premium.\n- The project is ready, or you can comfortably manage the possession timeline.\n- The total cost remains within your budget.\n\n## 4\\. Give me these details and I'll compare the two properly\n\nI need to know which two properties you're comparing before I can make a firm recommendation.\n\nResale society and sector\n\n[Input placeholder: e\\.g\\. Mahagun Moderne, Sector 78] (Current value unavailable.)\n\nNew project and sector\n\n[Input placeholder: e\\.g\\. project name, Sector 150] (Current value unavailable.)\n\nNew project's current status\n\nReady to move\n\nNear completion\n\nUnder construction\n\nAre the flat sizes comparable?\n\nSimilar carpet area\n\nNew project is larger\n\nResale is larger\n\nNot sure\n\nApproximate age of the resale society\n\nUnder 5 years\n\n5–7 years\n\n7–12 years\n\nOver 12 years\n\nNot sure\n\n[Compare my two options]\n\nWith those details, I can assess whether the new project genuinely justifies the extra ₹15 lakh or whether the resale offers better value and lower risk.",

        {

        {
            "id": "07f6ea5f-5b27-4209-9283-ea3c4bbe8098",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1792072316.210178,
            "update_time": 1792072360.504311,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "For a ₹1.65 crore resale flat in Noida, **don't pay a token or sign an unconditional agreement until you've verified ownership, the property's registry status, and all outstanding dues.** A flat can look perfect and still have problems with its title, the land lease, unpaid society charges or transfer eligibility.\n\nI'd divide your checks into two parts: documents proving you can legally buy the flat, and charges that could become your responsibility after purchase.\n\n## 1. Documents you must verify\n\n<box gap={3}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"file-check\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **1. Registered sale deed and ownership chain**\n\n      Ask for the seller's registered sale deed and previous title documents establishing the ownership chain. Confirm the seller's name, flat number, tower, floor, area and property identifiers match the actual flat. If the property was inherited, jointly owned or held through a power of attorney, get specialist legal advice on the additional documents required.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"landmark\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **2. Registry and Noida Authority records**\n\n      Establish whether the flat has a registered conveyance deed or sale deed, or whether it remains subject to a builder's allotment and transfer process. For a leasehold property, have your lawyer verify the lease deed, relevant authority permissions and transfer requirements. **An allotment letter alone is not equivalent to registered ownership.**\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"landmark\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **3. Encumbrance and loan checks**\n\n      Ask whether the flat has an outstanding home loan, mortgage, lien, court attachment or other restriction. Have a lawyer search the relevant registration records and available CERSAI records where applicable. If there is a loan, obtain the lender's outstanding statement and a documented process for repayment and release of the mortgage at closing.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"file-text\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **4. Occupancy and building approvals**\n\n      Request the applicable occupancy certificate (OC), completion certificate (CC), sanctioned building plan and evidence that the actual flat conforms to approved plans. Ask your lawyer to verify whether the project has any unresolved approval or deviation issues.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"receipt\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **5. Society and utility records**\n\n      Get a written no-dues certificate from the society or maintenance agency, recent maintenance bills, electricity and water bills, property-tax records where applicable, and confirmation of the allocated parking space. Check whether any special assessment or major repair levy has already been approved.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <box background=\"surface-secondary\" radius=\"lg\" size=\"40px\" align=\"center\" justify=\"center\">\n      <icon name=\"shield-check\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **6. Litigation and RERA checks**\n\n      Search for relevant disputes involving the flat, seller, builder and project. Check the project's RERA record where applicable, including disclosed completion details and complaints. A clean RERA page by itself does not establish clear title.\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## 2. Outstanding charges that could cost you extra\n\nAsk the seller and society for written statements, not just verbal assurances.\n\n| Charge or liability | What to ask for |\n|---|---|\n| Noida Authority dues | Any unpaid lease rent, land-related dues, transfer charges, interest or penalties |\n| Society maintenance | Outstanding monthly maintenance, late fees and any approved special levy |\n| Property tax | Latest paid receipts and confirmation of arrears, if applicable |\n| Electricity and water | Final bills, unpaid amounts, meter details and any connection-related dues |\n| Home loan | Current outstanding amount, lender's foreclosure statement and mortgage-release procedure |\n| Parking and transfer | Whether parking is legally allocated and whether society or authority transfer fees apply |\n| Major repairs | Any upcoming lift replacement, waterproofing, façade work, plumbing repairs or other major expenses |\n| Brokerage and documentation | Who pays the broker, lawyer, deed drafting and other agreed transaction expenses |\n\n**Pay special attention to Noida Authority dues.** Depending on the project's land and lease arrangements, an apartment's transfer process can involve authority-related documentation and charges. Your lawyer should establish exactly which approvals and payments apply to this property rather than relying on the seller's estimate.\n\nFor UP registration procedures and applicable stamp duty, consult the official [IGRS Uttar Pradesh portal](https://www.igrsup.gov.in/). <Cite refs={[\"turn809613search11\"]}/>\n\n## 3. Don't forget TDS on the ₹1.65 crore purchase\n\nIf the seller is an Indian tax resident, Section 194-IA generally requires you, as the buyer, to deduct **1% TDS on the consideration or stamp-duty value, whichever is higher**, subject to the statutory rules. At ₹1.65 crore, 1% of the quoted price alone is ₹1.65 lakh, assuming the stamp-duty value is not higher and no other applicable adjustment changes the calculation. This is deducted from the amount paid to the seller; it is not an extra ₹1.65 lakh added to the purchase price. If the seller is non-resident, different tax provisions apply. <Cite refs={[\"turn809613search0\",\"turn809613search48\"]}/>\n\nHave your CA confirm the correct treatment of the transaction, including the applicable filing process for the date of purchase.\n\n## 4. Use this checklist before paying a token\n\n{@body const checks = [\"Seller's registered deed and complete ownership chain verified by my lawyer\",\"Flat's registry, leasehold status and transfer eligibility confirmed\",\"Mortgage, litigation and other encumbrances checked\",\"Occupancy certificate and approved building plans reviewed\",\"Noida Authority dues and transfer requirements confirmed in writing\",\"Society maintenance no-dues certificate obtained\",\"Property tax, electricity and water bills checked\",\"Parking allocation and all additional charges confirmed\",\"Repair history and upcoming special assessments checked\",\"Written sale agreement includes clear title, dues and refund protections\"]}\n{@body const [done,setDone] = DIL.useState([])}\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={3} gap={3}>\n  <row align=\"center\" justify=\"between\">\n    <title size=\"lg\">Resale purchase checklist</title>\n    <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">{done.length} of {checks.length}</text>\n  </row>\n  <box background=\"surface-tertiary\" height=\"5px\" radius=\"full\" clip>\n    <box background=\"surface-inverted\" height=\"5px\" radius=\"full\" width={`${done.length/checks.length*100}%`}/>\n  </box>\n  {#each checks as item,i}\n    <checkbox key={item} checked={done.includes(i)} onChange={v=>setDone(old=>v?[...old,i]:old.filter(n=>n!==i))}>{item}</checkbox>\n  {/each}\n  <button block variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => GenUI.copy(checks.map((c,i)=>`${done.includes(i)?\"[x]\":\"[ ]\"} ${c}`).join(\"\\n\"))}><icon name=\"copy\" size=\"xs\" inline/> Copy checklist</button>\n</box>\n\nMy strongest advice is to appoint an independent Noida property lawyer who represents you, not the seller or broker. Have the lawyer review the documents and the draft sale agreement before any non-refundable payment.\n\nOne detail would help me make this more specific: **Is the flat already registered in the seller's name, or does it still have only a builder allotment letter or transfer paperwork pending?** That changes which documents and liabilities deserve the most attention."
                ]
